2026-11-20T17:33:53.8826521Z [36;1m# that added a certificate, beside its entry or alone, ran every[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8826844Z [36;1m# self-test: about an hour of CI per PR, for a suite in which a[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8827173Z [36;1m# few tests open anything under certs/. The certificate checker is[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:53.8829366Z [36;1m# entry.[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:53.8837258Z [36;1m#[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:53.8844151Z [36;1mslug_tests=""[0m

2026-11-20T17:33:53.8846183Z [36;1m                   -e 's|^certs/\(.*/\)\{0,1\}\([^/]*\)\.json$|\2|p' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8846657Z [36;1m                   -e 's|^repro/\([^/]*\)\.json$|\1|p' | sort -u)[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8846982Z [36;1mfor slug in $slugs; do[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8847303Z [36;1m  # Both spellings of one entry: the slug the file is named for,[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8847720Z [36;1m  # and the [[n,k,d]] form a test may cite instead. -F keeps a[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8848092Z [36;1m  # slug that looks like a pattern (and one that starts with a[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8848455Z [36;1m  # dash) from meaning anything to grep.[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8848868Z [36;1m  slug_tests="$slug_tests $(grep -rlF --include='test_*.py' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8849234Z [36;1m                -e "$slug" -e "${slug//-/,}" \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8849572Z [36;1m                research verify site cli || true)"[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8849892Z [36;1m  if [ -f "$GATE_GOLDENS/$slug.json" ]; then[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8850288Z [36;1m    slug_tests="$slug_tests verify/test_validation_gate.py"[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8850596Z [36;1m  fi[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8850837Z [36;1mdone[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8851106Z [36;1mslug_tests=$(printf '%s\n' $slug_tests \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8851551Z [36;1m               | grep -E '^(research|verify|site|cli)/([A-Za-z0-9_]+/)*test_[A-Za-z0-9_.]+\.py$' \[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8851963Z [36;1m               | sort -u | tr '\n' ' ' | sed 's/ *$//' || true)[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8852273Z [36;1mif [ -n "$slug_tests" ]; then[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8852692Z [36;1m  echo "self-tests naming an entry this PR changed: $slug_tests" | tee -a "$GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY"[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8853093Z [36;1mfi[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8853392Z [36;1m# A circuit.ler claim nothing in the PR touched can only go stale[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8853805Z [36;1m# through the stack that measures it: the measured-rate tier, the[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8854236Z [36;1m# decoder, the pinned dependencies (a stim bump in uv.lock) or the[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8854744Z [36;1m# accelerator build, which is the LER_STACK list in this job's env.[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8855311Z [36;1m# A PR that leaves all of those alone gets the --ler-base run; a PR[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8855687Z [36;1m# that touches any of them re-measures a sample of the claims[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8856104Z [36;1m# before it merges, and the weekly runs re-measure them all (the[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8856548Z [36;1m# sweep that passes --ler-sample below has the argument). Everything else[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8856975Z [36;1m# under verify/ (the structural verifier, the circuit tier, the[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8857410Z [36;1m# gate, the policy checkers, tests, fixtures, the integrity[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8857823Z [36;1m# manifest) runs on every entry in the --ler-base pass as well and[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8858222Z [36;1m# cannot change a measured rate, so it is left out. The list used[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8858597Z [36;1m# to be all of verify/ minus tests, checkers, and fixtures: a[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8858994Z [36;1m# prose-checker edit scheduled the full re-measure on PR #3418, and[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8859431Z [36;1m# once those were excluded a schema-check edit did on #4653 and[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8859809Z [36;1m# #4682, which with about an hour of self-tests runs past this[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8860199Z [36;1m# job's 130-minute timeout. A diff that cannot be computed exits[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8860602Z [36;1m# non-zero and so counts as changed (fail closed). $LER_STACK is[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8861017Z [36;1m# unquoted so that it splits into one pathspec per entry.[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8861410Z [36;1mif git diff --quiet "$base"...HEAD -- $LER_STACK; then[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8861754Z [36;1m  ler_stack_changed=false[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8862000Z [36;1melse[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8862214Z [36;1m  ler_stack_changed=true[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8862496Z [36;1mfi[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8862759Z [36;1mecho "codes_changed=$codes_changed" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"[0m
2026-11-20T17:33:53.8863200Z [36;1mecho "data_only=$data_only" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"[0m
